## 00_README

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Workbook | SGW SGU Human Starter / Prologue Dev Master v2 |
| Strict scope | Complete SGU Human opening route: SGC_W1 starter crisis → Dakara_E1 SG-18 visit → shared Dakara war/betrayal chain → temporary SGC support loop → Dakara climax → SGC Aftermath/Shutdown → first Omega Site arrival and Hammond-letter handoff. |
| SGC chain | 1559 → 1561 → 1562 → 1563 → 1564 → 1565 → 1566 → 1568 → 1569, with local auxiliary 1567 / 1571. |
| Dakara entry | 1570 SG-18 sends the Human from SGC to Dakara. Its dialogue set contains explicit Human/Tau'ri variants. |
| Dakara continuation | 1645-1653 is not Free-Jaffa-exclusive: recovered dialogue includes Human/Tau'ri variants. Mission1646 explicitly references the death of SG-18, and Mission1647 contains 'Moh'katan sends one of the Tau'ri to command us?'. |
| Omega convergence | 1654 Aftermath and 1655 Shutdown contain separate Human and Free-Jaffa variants. Human is told to deliver Hammond's message to Dr. Jackson; Free Jaffa is told to deliver it to Bra'tac. |
| Hard stop | SGU Human starter/prologue ends after Mission1655 Step4942 Objective6056: deliver Hammond's letter to Daniel Jackson at the Omega/Capitol-Ship command-center handoff. |
| Worlds in scope | SGC_W1 World58 → Dakara_E1 World61 (+ StoryRm62) → SGC_W1 → Omega_Site World18 → first Command Center handoff (Omega_Site_CmdCenter World80 exists; exact Capitol-Ship mapping still requires transition-script confirmation). |
| Map coverage | SGC_W1, Dakara_E1 and Omega Site production map files all survive in the client handoff; route file index is expanded accordingly. |
| Evidence labels | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED; USER-CONFIRMED; RECONSTRUCTION / INFERENCE; PARTIAL / UNRESOLVED / MISSING. |
| Authority rule | Raw cooked mission/dialog/interaction/world/gate records outrank earlier workbook scope assumptions. The earlier v1 stopped too early and is superseded by this v2. |
